  3379	def _raw_error_terminal(operation: Operation, node: ast.AST) -> bool:
  3380	    if not isinstance(node, ast.Return) or node.value is None:
  3381	        return False
  3382	    value = node.value
  3383	    if (
  3384	        isinstance(value, (ast.Tuple, ast.List))
  3385	        and value.elts
  3386	        and isinstance(value.elts[0], ast.Constant)
  3387	        and isinstance(value.elts[0].value, int)
  3388	        and 400 <= value.elts[0].value <= 599
  3389	    ):
  3390	        return True
  3391	    if not isinstance(value, ast.Call) or not isinstance(value.func, ast.Name):
  3392	        return False
  3393	    if value.func.id in operation.local_names:
  3394	        return False
  3395	    binding = operation.body_bindings.get(value.func.id)
  3396	    if binding is None or binding.kind != "symbol_import" or binding.origin not in RAW_RESPONSE_TYPES:
  3397	        return False
  3398	    for keyword in value.keywords:
  3399	        if (
  3400	            keyword.arg in {"status", "status_code"}
  3401	            and isinstance(keyword.value, ast.Constant)
  3402	            and isinstance(keyword.value.value, int)
  3403	            and 400 <= keyword.value.value <= 599
  3404	        ):
  3405	            return True
  3406	    return False

  3409	def _known_error_assignments(
  3410	    operation: Operation,
  3411	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3412	    analysis: list[str],
  3413	) -> dict[str, set[int]]:
  3414	    assignments: dict[str, set[int]] = {
  3415	        name: set() for name in _annotated_error_parameters(operation, language)
  3416	    }
  3417	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(operation.node.body):
  3418	        if not isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):
  3419	            continue
  3420	        name = _simple_assignment_target(node)
  3421	        value = _statement_value(node)
  3422	        if name is None or not isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  3423	            continue
  3424	        if _known_constructor(operation, value, language, analysis) is not None:
  3425	            assignments.setdefault(name, set()).add(id(value))
  3426	    return assignments
  3427

  3429	def _status_is_error_mapping(
  3430	    operation: Operation,
  3431	    call: ast.Call,
  3432	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3433	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3434	    analysis: list[str],
  3435	) -> bool:
  3436	    if not _status_call(operation, call, analysis) or len(call.args) < 2:
  3437	        return False
  3438	    second = call.args[1]
  3439	    if isinstance(second, ast.Name) and second.id in error_assignments:
  3440	        return True
  3441	    return isinstance(second, ast.Call) and _known_constructor(
  3442	        operation, second, language, analysis
  3443	    ) is not None

  3446	def _literal_error_status_call(
  3447	    operation: Operation,
  3448	    call: ast.Call,
  3449	    analysis: list[str],
  3450	) -> bool:
  3451	    if not _status_call(operation, call, analysis) or len(call.args) < 2:
  3452	        return False
  3453	    status = call.args[0]
  3454	    return (
  3455	        isinstance(status, ast.Constant)
  3456	        and isinstance(status.value, int)
  3457	        and 400 <= status.value <= 599
  3458	    )

  3461	def _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3462	    operation: Operation,
  3463	    statements: list[ast.stmt],
  3464	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3465	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3466	    analysis: list[str],
  3467	) -> bool:
  3468	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(statements):
  3469	        if isinstance(node, ast.Raise) or _raw_error_terminal(operation, node):
  3470	            return True
  3471	        if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  3472	            if _known_constructor(operation, node, language, analysis) is not None:
  3473	                return True
  3474	            if _status_is_error_mapping(
  3475	                operation, node, error_assignments, language, analysis
  3476	            ):
  3477	                return True
  3478	            if _literal_error_status_call(operation, node, analysis):
  3479	                return True
  3480	    return False

  3483	def _candidate_branch(
  3484	    operation: Operation,
  3485	    node: ast.AST,
  3486	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3487	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3488	    analysis: list[str],
  3489	) -> bool:
  3490	    if isinstance(node, ast.If):
  3491	        if _result_test_name(node.test) is not None:
  3492	            return True
  3493	        return _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3494	            operation,
  3495	            [*node.body, *node.orelse],
  3496	            language,
  3497	            error_assignments,
  3498	            analysis,
  3499	        )
  3500	    if MATCH is not None and isinstance(node, MATCH):
  3501	        bodies = [statement for case in node.cases for statement in case.body]
  3502	        return _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3503	            operation, bodies, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3504	        )
  3505	    return False
  3506	
  3507	
  3508	def _call_assignment(statement: ast.stmt) -> tuple[str, ast.Call] | None:
  3509	    name = _simple_assignment_target(statement)
  3510	    value = _statement_value(statement)
  3511	    if name is None or not isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  3512	        return None
  3513	    return name, value
  3514	
  3515	
  3516	def _try_assignment(statement: ast.stmt) -> tuple[str, ast.Call] | None:
  3517	    if not isinstance(statement, ast.Try) or len(statement.body) != 1:
  3518	        return None
  3519	    return _call_assignment(statement.body[0])
  3520	
  3521	
  3522	def _analyze_results(
  3523	    operation: Operation,
  3524	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3525	    analysis: list[str],
  3526	    findings: list[Finding],
  3527	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  3528	    allowed_error_calls: set[int],
  3529	    allowed_status_calls: set[int],
  3530	) -> None:
  3531	    body = _without_docstrings(operation.node.body)
  3532	    error_assignments = _known_error_assignments(operation, language, analysis)
  3533	    approved_branches: set[int] = set()
  3534	
  3535	    for index in range(len(body) - 1):
  3536	        assignment = _call_assignment(body[index])
  3537	        branch = body[index + 1]
  3538	        if assignment is None or not isinstance(branch, ast.If):
  3539	            continue
  3540	        if _known_constructor(operation, assignment[1], language, analysis) is not None:
  3541	            # A local FrameworkErrorSchema construction is mapping behavior, not the
  3542	            # application-call assignment that can causally own a Result arm.
  3543	            continue
  3544	        result_name, _ = assignment
  3545	        if not _candidate_branch(
  3546	            operation, branch, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3547	        ):
  3548	            continue
  3549	        if not _supported_result_test(operation, branch.test, result_name, analysis):
  3550	            analysis.append(
  3551	                f"{operation.parsed.relative_path}:{branch.lineno} Result candidate predicate 분석 불능"
  3552	            )
  3553	            continue
  3554	        approved_branches.add(id(branch))
  3555	        _validate_mapping_body(
  3556	            operation,
  3557	            branch.body,
  3558	            language,
  3559	            analysis,
  3560	            findings,
  3561	            seen,
  3562	            allowed_error_calls,
  3563	            allowed_status_calls,
  3564	            "Result arm must directly construct FrameworkErrorSchema and return Status",
  3565	        )
  3566	
  3567	    for index, statement in enumerate(body):
  3568	        if not isinstance(statement, ast.If):
  3569	            continue
  3570	        if id(statement) in approved_branches:
  3571	            continue
  3572	        if not _candidate_branch(
  3573	            operation, statement, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3574	        ):
  3575	            continue
  3576	        test_name = _result_test_name(statement.test)
  3577	        if test_name is None:
  3578	            analysis.append(
  3579	                f"{operation.parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} unsupported Result/error candidate predicate"
  3580	            )
  3581	            continue
  3582	        previous = body[index - 1] if index else None
  3583	        dual = _try_assignment(previous) if previous is not None else None
  3584	        if dual is not None and dual[0] == test_name:
  3585	            _append_finding(
  3586	                findings,
  3587	                seen,
  3588	                operation.parsed,
  3589	                statement,
  3590	                "same call cannot use exception and Result mapping",
  3591	                requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3592	            )
  3593	            continue
  3594	        direct = _call_assignment(previous) if previous is not None else None
  3595	        if direct is not None and direct[0] != test_name:
  3596	            if _known_constructor(operation, direct[1], language, analysis) is not None:
  3597	                _append_finding(
  3598	                    findings,
  3599	                    seen,
  3600	                    operation.parsed,
  3601	                    statement,
  3602	                    "orphan/pre-call error mapping is not causally owned by an application call",
  3603	                    requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3604	                )
  3605	            else:
  3606	                analysis.append(
  3607	                    f"{operation.parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} Result candidate references another variable"
  3608	                )
  3609	            continue
  3610	        _append_finding(
  3611	            findings,
  3612	            seen,
  3613	            operation.parsed,
  3614	            statement,
  3615	            "Result/error mapping must immediately follow its try-free call assignment",
  3616	            requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3617	        )
  3618	
  3619	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(operation.node.body):
  3620	        if MATCH is not None and isinstance(node, MATCH):
  3621	            if _candidate_branch(
  3622	                operation, node, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3623	            ):
  3624	                analysis.append(
  3625	                    f"{operation.parsed.relative_path}:{node.lineno} unsupported Result/error match candidate"
  3626	                )
  3627	
  3628	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(operation.node.body):
  3629	        if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  3630	            continue
  3631	        constructor = _known_constructor(operation, node, language, analysis)
  3632	        if constructor is not None and id(node) not in allowed_error_calls:
  3633	            _append_finding(
  3634	                findings,
  3635	                seen,
  3636	                operation.parsed,
  3637	                node,
  3638	                "FrameworkErrorSchema construction is not owned by an approved catch/Result arm",
  3639	                requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3640	            )
  3641	        error_status = _status_is_error_mapping(
  3642	            operation, node, error_assignments, language, analysis
  3643	        ) or _literal_error_status_call(operation, node, analysis)
  3644	        if error_status and id(node) not in allowed_status_calls:
  3645	            _append_finding(
  3646	                findings,
  3647	                seen,
  3648	                operation.parsed,
  3649	                node,
  3650	                "error Status mapping is not owned by an approved catch/Result arm",
  3651	                requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3652	            )
  3653

  6806	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
  6807	    try:
  6808	        config = _parse_config(argv[1:])
  6809	    except UsageError as exc:
  6810	        print(
  6811	            f"[check-api-error-controller-contract] 사용 오류: {exc}",
  6812	            file=sys.stderr,
  6813	        )
  6814	        return 1
  6815	    except SystemExit as exc:
  6816	        return int(exc.code)
  6817	
  6818	    if config.anchor is not None:
  6819	        # 재료 선검증 — 무발견 clean 이라도 resolve 불능 앵커·부재/형식 오류 빚 파일·
  6820	        # 공허 차분이 침묵 exit 0 되지 않게 parse 직후 막는다(fail-closed).
  6821	        try:
  6822	            anchor_diff.validate_materials(config.root, config.anchor, config.anchor_debt_file)
  6823	        except anchor_diff.AnchorDiffUsage as exc:
  6824	            print(
  6825	                f"[check-api-error-controller-contract] 사용 오류: {exc}",
  6826	                file=sys.stderr,
  6827	            )
  6828	            return 1
  6829	
  6830	    # 표준 트리 슬라이스 — 프로필과 무관하게 먼저 본다(옛 auto 무동작 = fail-open 차단).
  6831	    bcs = _tree_bcs2(config.root)
  6832	    if not any((bc / d).is_dir() for bc in bcs for d in _TREE_DRIVING) and _tree_adopted2(bcs):
  6833	        print("[check-api-error-controller-contract] blocker: 채택 신호는 있는데 driving 층이 0건이다 — 조용한 무동작을 금지한다(#74)")
  6834	        return 2
  6835	    # --anchor 미지정이면 현행 그대로 각 슬라이스에서 즉시 exit 2 — 지정 시에만
  6836	    # 슬라이스 진단을 모아 마지막에 판정 차분(anchor_diff)으로 exit 를 정한다.
  6837	    collected: list[str] = []
  6838	    pending_analysis: list[str] = []
  6839	    tree_findings, tree_candidates = _tree_slice2(config.root, bcs)
  6840	    if tree_findings:
  6841	        print("[check-api-error-controller-contract] BLOCKER — 입구(컨트롤러) 계약 규율 위반 (트리 8·11·12행):")
  6842	        for f in tree_findings:
  6843	            print(f)
  6844	    if tree_candidates:
  6845	        print("[check-api-error-controller-contract] ⓓ 후보 — 기계가 후보를 좁혔다 · 마무리 물음은 discipline-reviewer 몫(exit 불산입):")
  6846	        for c in tree_candidates:
  6847	            print(c)
  6848	    if tree_findings:
  6849	        if config.anchor is None:
  6850	            return 2
  6851	        collected.extend(tree_findings)
  6852	
  6853	    if config.profile != "auto":
  6854	        try:
  6855	            analysis, findings = _run(config)
  6856	            dynamic_proof_only = bool(analysis) and all(

  6858	                for issue in analysis

  6872	                # 레코드는 필터 통과 후 «최종 집합»의 거울이다 — 라인 문면은 render()
  6873	                # 소유 그대로 두고 add 만 얹는다(dedupe·필터·exit 경로 무변). 두 sink 를
  6874	                # 나눠 출력하면 findings 순서가 섞이므로 인쇄는 이 단일 루프가 유지한다.

  6899	                    "path and direct two-argument Status(<approved HTTP status>, error); helper/handler/raw "
  6900	                    "detours are not part of this contract."
  6901	                )
  6902	                if config.anchor is None:
  6903	                    return 2
  6904	                collected.extend(finding.render() for finding in reported_findings)
  6905	                # 토큰-only analysis 는 차분 강등으로 소거되지 않는다 — findings 가
  6906	                # 전건 앵커 기존분이어도 proof 경로(exit 1)로 남긴다(fail-open 차단).
  6907	                pending_analysis = analysis
  6908	            elif analysis:
  6909	                raise UsageError("; ".join(analysis))
  6910	        except UsageError as exc:

  2199	        return name.startswith(("__get_pydantic_", "__pydantic_")) or name in {
  2200	            "__get_validators__",
  2201	            "__modify_schema__",
  2202	        }

  2204	    def contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2205	        value: ast.AST | None,
  2206	        bindings: dict[str, str],
  2207	    ) -> bool:
  2208	        if value is None:
  2209	            return False
  2210	        for candidate in ast.walk(value):
  2211	            if not isinstance(candidate, ast.Call):
  2212	                continue
  2213	            resolved = _resolve(candidate.func, bindings)
  2214	            if resolved in SCHEMA_DECORATORS:
  2215	                return True
  2216	            if (
  2217	                resolved is not None
  2218	                and resolved.startswith("pydantic.")
  2219	                and resolved.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in SCHEMA_DECORATOR_TAILS
  2220	            ):
  2221	                return True
  2222	        return False
  2223	
  2224	    if not allow_model_config:
  2225	        for decorator in node.decorator_list:
  2226	            _append_finding(
  2227	                findings,
  2228	                seen,
  2229	                parsed,

  2231	                "class decorator outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",

  2233	        for keyword in node.keywords:
  2234	            _append_finding(

  2239	                "class keyword config outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2240	            )

  2275	                    else:
  2276	                        _append_finding(
  2277	                            findings,
  2278	                            seen,
  2279	                            parsed,
  2280	                            statement,
  2281	                            "dynamic ClassVar assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2282	                        )
  2283	                continue
  2284	            if name.startswith("_"):
  2285	                if not allow_model_config and contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2286	                    statement.value,
  2287	                    bindings,
  2288	                ):
  2289	                    _append_finding(
  2290	                        findings,
  2291	                        seen,
  2292	                        parsed,
  2293	                        statement,
  2294	                        "schema decorator proxy outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2295	                    )
  2296	                elif (
  2297	                    not allow_model_config
  2298	                    and _static_assignment_binding(statement.value, bindings) is None
  2299	                ):
  2300	                    _append_finding(
  2301	                        findings,
  2302	                        seen,
  2303	                        parsed,
  2304	                        statement,
  2305	                        "dynamic private class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2306	                    )
  2307	                continue
  2308	            if name in field_names:
  2309	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "duplicate public field")
  2310	            field_names.add(name)
  2311	            if name not in allowed_fields:
  2312	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "additional public field")
  2313	        elif isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
  2314	            names = {name for target in statement.targets for name in _target_names(target)}
  2315	            if not names and not allow_model_config:
  2316	                _append_finding(
  2317	                    findings,
  2318	                    seen,
  2319	                    parsed,
  2320	                    statement,
  2321	                    "complex class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2322	                )
  2323	            for name in names:
  2324	                if not allow_model_config and pydantic_hook(name):
  2325	                    _append_finding(
  2326	                        findings,
  2327	                        seen,
  2328	                        parsed,
  2329	                        statement,
  2330	                        "Pydantic hook override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2331	                    )
  2332	                elif name == "model_config":
  2333	                    if not allow_model_config:
  2334	                        _append_finding(
  2335	                            findings,
  2336	                            seen,
  2337	                            parsed,
  2338	                            statement,
  2339	                            "model_config override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2340	                        )
  2341	                elif not name.startswith("_"):
  2342	                    _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "public class assignment/helper")
  2343	                elif not allow_model_config and contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2344	                    statement.value,
  2345	                    bindings,
  2346	                ):
  2347	                    _append_finding(
  2348	                        findings,
  2349	                        seen,
  2350	                        parsed,
  2351	                        statement,
  2352	                        "schema decorator proxy outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2353	                    )
  2354	            if (
  2355	                not allow_model_config
  2356	                and names
  2357	                and all(name.startswith("_") for name in names)
  2358	                and _static_assignment_binding(statement.value, bindings) is None
  2359	            ):
  2360	                _append_finding(
  2361	                    findings,
  2362	                    seen,
  2363	                    parsed,
  2364	                    statement,
  2365	                    "dynamic private class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2366	                )
  2367	        elif isinstance(statement, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  2368	            decorated_schema_method = any(
  2369	                _resolve(
  2370	                    decorator.func if isinstance(decorator, ast.Call) else decorator,
  2371	                    bindings,
  2372	                )
  2373	                in SCHEMA_DECORATORS
  2374	                for decorator in statement.decorator_list
  2375	            )
  2376	            if not allow_schema_decorators or (
  2377	                not decorated_schema_method and not statement.name.startswith("_")
  2378	            ):
  2379	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "validator/public helper")
  2380	        elif (
  2381	            isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef)
  2382	            and not statement.name.startswith("_")
  2383	            and not (allow_model_config and statement.name == "Config")
  2384	        ):
  2385	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "public nested class/helper")
  2386	        elif (
  2387	            isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef)
  2388	            and allow_model_config
  2389	            and statement.name == "Config"
  2390	        ) or isinstance(statement, ast.Pass) or (
  2391	            isinstance(statement, ast.Expr)
  2392	            and isinstance(statement.value, ast.Constant)
  2393	            and isinstance(statement.value.value, str)
  2394	        ):
  2395	            continue
  2396	        else:
  2397	            if allow_model_config:
  2398	                analysis.append(
  2399	                    "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  2400	                    f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  2401	                    "common FrameworkErrorSchema executable class-body statement는 target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  2402	                )
  2403	            else:
  2404	                _append_finding(
  2405	                    findings,
  2406	                    seen,
  2407	                    parsed,
  2408	                    statement,
  2409	                    "executable class-body statement outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2410	                )
  2411	
  2412	
  2413	def _assignment_value(statement: ast.stmt) -> ast.AST | None:
  2414	    if isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
  2415	        return statement.value
  2416	    if isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign):
  2417	        return statement.value
  2418	    return None
  2419	
  2420	
  2421	def _is_functional_enum(
  2422	    statement: ast.stmt, bindings: dict[str, str]
  2423	) -> bool:
  2424	    value = _assignment_value(statement)
  2425	    return (
  2426	        isinstance(value, ast.Call)
  2427	        and _resolve(value.func, bindings) in ENUM_BASES
  2428	    )
  2429	
  2430	
  2431	def _bc_module_artifact_findings(
  2432	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  2433	    allowed_classes: set[ast.ClassDef],
  2434	    findings: list[Finding],
  2435	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  2436	) -> None:
  2437	    """Keep the canonical BC error module declarative and mutation-free."""
  2438	
  2439	    before = _module_bindings(parsed)
  2440	    for statement in parsed.tree.body:
  2441	        if isinstance(statement, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
  2442	            continue
  2443	        if isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef):
  2444	            if statement not in allowed_classes:
  2445	                _append_finding(
  2446	                    findings,
  2447	                    seen,
  2448	                    parsed,
  2449	                    statement,
  2450	                    "BC error module extra class/helper forbidden",
  2451	                )
  2452	            continue
  2453	        if (
  2454	            isinstance(statement, ast.Expr)
  2455	            and isinstance(statement.value, ast.Constant)
  2456	            and isinstance(statement.value.value, str)
  2457	        ):
  2458	            continue
  2459	        if isinstance(statement, ast.Assign) and len(statement.targets) == 1:
  2460	            target = statement.targets[0]
  2461	            value = statement.value
  2462	        elif isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign):
  2463	            target = statement.target
  2464	            value = statement.value
  2465	        else:
  2466	            target = None
  2467	            value = None
  2468	        bindings = before.get(id(statement), {})
  2469	        if (
  2470	            isinstance(target, ast.Name)
  2471	            and target.id.startswith("_")
  2472	            and _static_assignment_binding(value, bindings) is not None
  2473	        ):
  2474	            continue
  2475	        _append_finding(
  2476	            findings,
  2477	            seen,
  2478	            parsed,
  2479	            statement,
  2480	            "BC error module helper/mutation/side effect forbidden",
  2481	        )
  2482	
  2483	
  2484	COMMON_CONFIG_MUTATION_METHODS = {
  2485	    "__delitem__",
  2486	    "__init__",
  2487	    "__ior__",
  2488	    "__setitem__",
  2489	    "clear",
  2490	    "pop",
  3005	        names = _static_enum_ignore_names(_assignment_value(statement))
  3006	        if names is None:
  3007	            # canonical BC ErrorCode 컨테이너의 dynamic 멤버 집합 — 일반 분석 오류가
  3008	            # 아니라 «동적 error shape» 유형이다(2026-08-15 r2″ 토큰 커버리지 확장):
  3009	            # wire 멤버 목록이 정적으로 안 서므로 2-리뷰어 runtime proof 경로에 태운다.
  3010	            dynamic = True
  3011	            analysis.append(
  3012	                "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  3013	                f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  3014	                "dynamic Enum _ignore_ 정적 분석 불능 — target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  3015	            )
  3016	        else:
  3017	            ignored.update(names)
  3018	    return ignored, dynamic
  3019	
  3020	
  3021	def _enum_members(
  3022	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  3023	    node: ast.ClassDef,
  3024	    analysis: list[str],
  3025	    findings: list[Finding],
  3026	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  3027	) -> tuple[dict[str, str], bool]:
  3028	    """(멤버 사전, 사전이 동적 해석 불능으로 «불완전»한가) — 둘째 값이 참이면
  3029	    멤버 사전 종속 판정(discriminator default 대조)은 파생 오탐이 되므로
  3030	    requires_static_error_shape 로 억제하고 runtime proof 가 대신 검증한다."""
  3031	    members: dict[str, str] = {}
  3032	    member_names: set[str] = set()
  3033	    ignored_names, dynamic = _enum_ignored_names(parsed, node, analysis)
  3034	    for statement in node.body:
  3035	        names: list[str] = []
  3036	        value: ast.AST | None = None
  3037	        if isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
  3038	            if all(isinstance(target, ast.Name) for target in statement.targets):
  3039	                names = [target.id for target in statement.targets]
  3040	            elif any(
  3041	                not _enum_reserved_name(name) and name not in ignored_names
  3042	                for target in statement.targets
  3043	                for name in _target_names(target)
  3044	            ):
  3045	                # canonical BC ErrorCode 의 비정형 멤버 대입 — «동적 error shape» 유형
  3046	                # (wire 멤버 모양이 정적으로 안 섬 · r2″ 토큰 커버리지 확장).
  3047	                dynamic = True
  3048	                analysis.append(
  3049	                    "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  3050	                    f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  3051	                    "dynamic Enum member shape 정적 분석 불능 — target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  3052	                )
  3053	            value = statement.value
  3054	        elif isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(statement.target, ast.Name):
  3055	            names = [statement.target.id]
  3056	            value = statement.value
  3057	        elif isinstance(statement, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  3058	            if not statement.name.startswith("_"):
  3059	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "Enum public helper forbidden")
  3060	            continue
  3061	        elif isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef):
  3062	            if not statement.name.startswith("_"):
  3063	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "Enum public helper forbidden")
  3064	            continue
  3065	        enum_names = [
  3066	            name
  3067	            for name in names
  3068	            if not _enum_reserved_name(name) and name not in ignored_names
  3069	        ]
  3070	        for name in enum_names:
  3071	            if value is None:
  3072	                continue
  3073	            if name in member_names:
  3074	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "duplicate Enum member")
  3075	            member_names.add(name)
  3076	            if not isinstance(value, ast.Constant) or not isinstance(value.value, str):
  3077	                # canonical BC ErrorCode 멤버의 비-리터럴 wire 값(f-string·상수 참조 등) —
  3078	                # 일반 분석 오류로 죽지 않고 «동적 error shape» 토큰을 발화해 2-리뷰어
  3079	                # runtime proof 경로에 태운다(2026-08-15 r2″ — 이 케이스가 exit 1 로
  3080	                # 죽어 proof 경로가 막혔던 토큰 커버리지 구멍의 실증 축).
  3081	                dynamic = True
  3082	                analysis.append(
  3083	                    "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  3084	                    f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  3085	                    f"dynamic Enum value 정적 분석 불능({name}) — target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  3086	                )
  3087	                continue
  3088	            members[name] = value.value
  3089	            if not WIRE_CODE_RE.fullmatch(value.value):
  3090	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "wire code must be snake_case")
  3091	    if not member_names:
  3092	        _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, "ErrorCode requires a wire-code member")
  3093	    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
  3094	    for value in members.values():
  3095	        counts[value] = counts.get(value, 0) + 1
  3096	    for value, count in counts.items():
  3097	        if count > 1:
  3098	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, f"duplicate wire code in Enum: {value}")
  3099	    return members, dynamic
  3100	
  3101	
  3102	def _analyze_bc_module(
  3103	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  3104	    bc: str,
  3105	    common_contracts: dict[str, CommonFieldContract],
  3106	    analysis: list[str],
  3107	    findings: list[Finding],
  3108	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  3109	    project_module_prefixes: set[str],
  3110	) -> tuple[
  3111	    dict[str, str],
  3112	    set[str],
  3113	    str,
  3114	    str | None,
  3115	    frozenset[str],
  3116	    frozenset[tuple[str | int, ...]],
  3117	    bool,
  3118	]:
  3119	    before = _module_bindings(parsed)
  3120	    for statement in _project_import_nodes(
  3121	        parsed,
  3122	        project_module_prefixes,
  3123	        allowed_modules=frozenset({COMMON_ERROR_MODULE}),
  3124	        recursive=True,
  3125	    ):
  3126	        analysis.append(
  3127	            "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  3128	            f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  3129	            "BC ErrorSchema의 추가 project/relative import는 target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  3130	        )
  3131	    prefix = _snake_to_pascal(bc)
  3132	    enum_name = f"{prefix}ErrorCode"
  3133	    base_name = f"{prefix}ErrorSchema"
  3134	    module = _module_name(parsed.relative_path)
  3135	    enum_full = f"{module}.{enum_name}"
  3136	    base_full = f"{module}.{base_name}"
  3137	    common_fields = set(common_contracts)
  3138	    classes = [node for node in parsed.tree.body if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef)]
  3139	
  3140	    enums = [node for node in classes if node.name == enum_name]
  3141	    if len(enums) != 1:
  3142	        _append_finding(
  3143	            findings,
  3144	            seen,
  3145	            parsed,
  3146	            enums[0] if enums else parsed.tree,
  3147	            f"exactly one {enum_name} required",
  3148	        )
  3149	    for node in classes:
  3150	        bindings = before.get(id(node), {})
  3151	        is_str_enum = any(_resolve(base, bindings) == STR_ENUM for base in node.bases)
  3152	        if node not in enums and (node.name.endswith("ErrorCode") or is_str_enum):
  3153	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, "second ErrorCode/StrEnum container")
  3154	    for node in parsed.tree.body:
  3155	        if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):
  3156	            bindings = before.get(id(node), {})
  3157	            if _is_functional_enum(node, bindings):
  3158	                _append_finding(
  3159	                    findings,
  3160	                    seen,
  3161	                    parsed,
  3162	                    node,
  3163	                    "second ErrorCode/StrEnum container",
  3164	                )
  3165	
  3166	    members: dict[str, str] = {}
  3167	    members_dynamic: bool = False
  3168	    if len(enums) == 1:
  3169	        enum = enums[0]
  3170	        bindings = before.get(id(enum), {})
  3171	        _base_contract(
  3172	            parsed,
  3173	            enum,
  3174	            bindings,
  3175	            STR_ENUM,
  3176	            "StrEnum",
  3177	            analysis,
  3178	            findings,
  3179	            seen,
  3180	            f"{enum_name} must directly inherit enum.StrEnum",
  3181	        )
  3182	        members, members_dynamic = _enum_members(parsed, enum, analysis, findings, seen)
  3183	
  3184	    bases = [node for node in classes if node.name == base_name]
  3185	    allowed_class_nodes: set[ast.ClassDef] = {*enums, *bases}
  3186	    if len(bases) != 1:
  3187	        _append_finding(
  3188	            findings,
  3189	            seen,
  3190	            parsed,
  3191	            bases[0] if bases else parsed.tree,
  3192	            f"exactly one {base_name} required",
  3193	        )
  3194	    for node in classes:
  3195	        bindings = before.get(id(node), {})
  3196	        direct_common = any(_resolve(base, bindings) == COMMON_ERROR_OUT for base in node.bases)
  3197	        if node not in bases and direct_common:
  3198	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, "second BC FrameworkErrorSchema base")
  3199	
  3200	    discriminator_field_name: str | None = None
  3201	    discriminator_constructor_keys: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
  3202	    discriminator_constructor_paths: frozenset[tuple[str | int, ...]] = (
  3203	        frozenset()
  3204	    )
  3205	    discriminator_constructor_keys_complete = True
  3206	    base_discriminator_default_member: str | None = None
  3207	    if len(bases) == 1:
  3208	        base = bases[0]
  3209	        bindings = before.get(id(base), {})
  3210	        _base_contract(
  3211	            parsed,
  3212	            base,
  3213	            bindings,
  3214	            COMMON_ERROR_OUT,
  3215	            "FrameworkErrorSchema",
  3216	            analysis,
  3217	            findings,
  3218	            seen,
  3219	            f"{base_name} must directly inherit common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  3220	        )
  3221	        fields = _public_annassigns(parsed, base, bindings)
  3222	        field_bindings = _class_body_bindings(parsed, base, bindings)
  3223	        discriminator_fields = []
  3224	        for field_name, field in fields.items():
  3225	            bindings_at_field = field_bindings.get(id(field), bindings)
  3226	            if _is_scalar_discriminator_annotation(
  3227	                field.annotation,
  3228	                bindings_at_field,
  3229	                {enum_full},
  3230	            ):
  3231	                discriminator_fields.append((field_name, field))
  3232	        if len(fields) != 1 or len(discriminator_fields) != 1:
  3233	            _append_finding(
  3234	                findings,
  3235	                seen,
  3236	                parsed,
  3237	                base,
  3238	                "BC FrameworkErrorSchema base must narrow exactly one common field to own ErrorCode",
  3239	            )
  3240	        if len(discriminator_fields) == 1:
  3241	            discriminator_field_name, discriminator_field = discriminator_fields[0]
  3242	            discriminator_bindings = field_bindings.get(
  3243	                id(discriminator_field), bindings
  3244	            )
  3245	            discriminator_constructor_keys = _field_constructor_keys(
  3246	                discriminator_field,
  3247	                discriminator_bindings,
  3248	            )
  3249	            discriminator_constructor_paths = _field_constructor_paths(
  3250	                discriminator_field,
  3251	                discriminator_bindings,
  3252	            )
  3253	            _record_unresolved_field_contract(
  3254	                parsed,
  3255	                discriminator_field,
  3256	                discriminator_bindings,
  3257	                analysis,
  3258	            )
  3259	            if discriminator_field_name not in common_fields:
  3260	                _append_finding(
  3261	                    findings,
  3262	                    seen,
  3263	                    parsed,
  3264	                    discriminator_field,
  3265	                    "BC FrameworkErrorSchema discriminator must override common field",
  3266	                )
  3267	            else:
  3268	                contract = common_contracts[discriminator_field_name]
  3269	                discriminator_constructor_keys_complete = (
  3270	                    contract.constructor_keys_complete
  3271	                )
  3272	                discriminator_constructor_keys = frozenset(
  3273	                    set(discriminator_constructor_keys)
  3274	                    | set(contract.constructor_keys)
  3275	                )
  3276	                discriminator_constructor_paths = frozenset(
  3277	                    set(discriminator_constructor_paths)
  3278	                    | set(contract.constructor_paths)
  3279	                )
  3280	                narrowed_signature = _annotation_signature(
  3281	                    discriminator_field.annotation,
  3282	                    discriminator_bindings,
  3283	                    replacements={enum_full: "<bc-error-code>"},
  3284	                )
  3285	                if (
  3286	                    contract.discriminator_annotation is None
  3287	                    or narrowed_signature != contract.discriminator_annotation
  3288	                ):
  3289	                    _append_finding(
  3290	                        findings,
  3291	                        seen,
  3292	                        parsed,
  3293	                        discriminator_field,
  3294	                        "BC base must preserve common annotation/nullability while narrowing str to own ErrorCode",
  3295	                    )
  3296	                if (
  3297	                    _field_metadata_signature(
  3298	                        discriminator_field,
  3299	                        discriminator_bindings,
  3300	                    )
  3301	                    != contract.metadata
  3302	                ):
  3303	                    _append_finding(
  3304	                        findings,
  3305	                        seen,
  3306	                        parsed,
  3307	                        discriminator_field,
  3308	                        "BC base field metadata must match common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  3309	                    )
  3310	                discriminator_default = _field_default_expression(
  3311	                    discriminator_field,
  3312	                    discriminator_bindings,
  3313	                )
  3314	                # 승인 canon(2026-08-15): 식별자 field 를 자기 ErrorCode 로 «정확히»
  3315	                # 좁히면서 공통의 default 를 잃어 required 가 되는 모양은 위반이 아니다.
  3316	                # 면제는 세 조건 동시 충족일 때만 — ① 이 field 가 공통 식별자 field 이고
  3317	                # ② annotation 좁힘이 공통 계약과 정확히 일치하며(ErrorCode 좁힘 동반)
  3318	                # ③ 공통엔 default 가 있고 base 는 default 없이 required 다. 그 밖의
  3319	                # required/default 의미 변화(default 값·kind 드리프트, 역방향 default
  3320	                # 추가)는 계속 위반이다.
  3321	                narrowed_required_canon: bool = (
  3322	                    contract.discriminator_annotation is not None
  3323	                    and narrowed_signature == contract.discriminator_annotation
  3324	                    and not contract.required
  3325	                    and _field_default_kind(
  3326	                        discriminator_field,
  3327	                        discriminator_bindings,
  3328	                    )
  3329	                    == "required"
  3330	                )
  3331	                if (
  3332	                    _field_default_signature(
  3333	                        discriminator_field,
  3334	                        discriminator_bindings,
  3335	                        enum_full=enum_full,
  3336	                        enum_members=members,
  3337	                    )
  3338	                    != contract.default
  3339	                    and not narrowed_required_canon
  3340	                ):
  3341	                    _append_finding(
  3342	                        findings,
  3343	                        seen,
  3344	                        parsed,
  3345	                        discriminator_field,
  3346	                        "BC base must preserve common required/default semantics",
  3347	                    )
  3348	                if isinstance(discriminator_default, ast.Constant) and isinstance(
  3349	                    discriminator_default.value,
  3350	                    str,
  3351	                ):
  3352	                    _append_finding(
  3353	                        findings,
  3354	                        seen,
  3355	                        parsed,
  3356	                        discriminator_field,
  3357	                        "raw string FrameworkErrorSchema discriminator",
  3358	                    )
  3359	                base_discriminator_default_member = _enum_member_name(
  3360	                    discriminator_default,
  3361	                    discriminator_bindings,
  3362	                    enum_full,
  3363	                    members,
  3364	                )
  3365	                if (
  3366	                    _field_default_kind(
  3367	                        discriminator_field,
  3368	                        discriminator_bindings,
  3369	                    )
  3370	                    != "required"
  3371	                    and not _default_is_none(
  3372	                        discriminator_default,
  3373	                        discriminator_bindings,
  3374	                    )
  3375	                    and base_discriminator_default_member is None
  3376	                ):
  3377	                    # 멤버 사전(members) 종속 판정 — 사전이 동적으로 «불완전»할 때만
  3378	                    # 파생 오탐이 되므로 그때만 proof 위임(정적 사전에선 진짜 위반).
  3379	                    _append_finding(
  3380	                        findings,
  3381	                        seen,
  3382	                        parsed,
  3383	                        discriminator_field,
  3384	                        "BC base discriminator default must be own ErrorCode member or None",
  3385	                        requires_static_error_shape=members_dynamic,
  3386	                    )
  3387	        _class_member_findings(
  3388	            parsed,
  3389	            base,
  3390	            bindings,
  3391	            {discriminator_field_name} if discriminator_field_name is not None else set(),
  3392	            analysis,
  3393	            findings,
  3394	            seen,
  3395	        )
  3396	
  3397	    known_concrete: set[str] = set()
  3398	    for node in classes:
  3399	        bindings = before.get(id(node), {})
  3400	        is_str_enum = any(_resolve(base, bindings) == STR_ENUM for base in node.bases)
  3401	        direct_common = any(_resolve(base, bindings) == COMMON_ERROR_OUT for base in node.bases)
  3402	        invalid_container = (
  3403	            node.name.endswith("ErrorCode")
  3404	            or is_str_enum
  3405	            or direct_common
  4480	    out: list[Path] = []
  4481	    for c in root.rglob("application"):
  4482	        if not c.is_dir() or set(c.parts) & CODE_SKIP_DIRS:
  4483	            continue
  4484	        out.extend(p for p in sorted(c.iterdir()) if p.is_dir() and not p.name.startswith("."))
  4485	    return out

  4487	
  4488	def _tree_adopted(bcs: list[Path]) -> bool:
  4489	    for bc in bcs:
  4490	        if any((bc / n).is_dir() for n in _TREE_LAYERS):
  4491	            return True
  4492	        if any((bc / m).is_file() for m in _TREE_APP_MARKERS):
  4493	            return True
  4494	        if any(p.is_dir() and p.name.startswith("django_") for p in bc.iterdir()):
  4495	            return True
  4496	    return False
  4497

  4499	def _tree_camel(bc_name: str) -> str:
  4500	    return "".join(part.capitalize() for part in bc_name.split("_"))
  4501	
  4502	
  4503	def _tree_slice(root: Path, bcs: list[Path]) -> list[str]:
  4504	    # 라인 문면은 호출자(이 함수) 소유 그대로 — 레코드만 실규칙 rule 로 함께 나간다.
  4505	    findings: SliceFindings = SliceFindings()
  4506	    for bc in bcs:
  4507	        expected_prefix = _tree_camel(bc.name)
  4508	        schema_files: list[Path] = []
  4509	        for driving in _TREE_DRIVING:
  4510	            api = bc / driving / "api"
  4511	            if not api.is_dir():
  4512	                continue
  4513	            api_rel = api.relative_to(root)
  4514	            schema_file = api / "bc_error_schema.py"
  4515	            if schema_file.is_file():
  4516	                schema_files.append(schema_file)
  4517	            else:
  4518	                findings.add(
  4519	                    rule="#114",
  4520	                    line=f"  [#114] {api_rel}: `bc_error_schema.py` 가 없다 — HTTP 오류를 아직 안 여는 BC 에도 «빈 파일»로 항상 있다(부모 api/ 가 항상이라)",
  4521	                    where=api_rel,
  4522	                    msg="`bc_error_schema.py` 가 없다 — HTTP 오류를 아직 안 여는 BC 에도 «빈 파일»로 항상 있다(부모 api/ 가 항상이라)",
  4523	                )
  4524	            for p in sorted(api.glob("*.py")):
  4525	                low = p.name.lower()
  4526	                if p.name != "bc_error_schema.py" and "error" in low and "schema" in low:
  4527	                    findings.add(
  4528	                        rule="#568",
  4529	                        line=f"  [#568] {p.relative_to(root)}: 폴더 밖 이름은 «접미» 자다 — BC 오류 스키마 파일은 `bc_error_schema.py` 하나다",
  4530	                        where=p.relative_to(root),
  4531	                        msg="폴더 밖 이름은 «접미» 자다 — BC 오류 스키마 파일은 `bc_error_schema.py` 하나다",
  4532	                    )
  4533	            for schema_dir in api.rglob("schema"):
  4534	                if not schema_dir.is_dir():
  4535	                    continue
  4536	                for p in sorted(schema_dir.glob("*error*.py")):
  4537	                    findings.add(
  4538	                        rule="#568",
  4539	                        line=f"  [#568] {p.relative_to(root)}: `schema/` 안 이름은 «접두» 자(schema_in/schema_out)다 — 오류 스키마는 `api/bc_error_schema.py` 로",
  4540	                        where=p.relative_to(root),
  4541	                        msg="`schema/` 안 이름은 «접두» 자(schema_in/schema_out)다 — 오류 스키마는 `api/bc_error_schema.py` 로",
  4542	                    )
  4543	        if len(schema_files) > 1:
  4544	            findings.add(
  4545	                rule="#114",
  4546	                line=f"  [#114] {bc.relative_to(root)}: `bc_error_schema.py` 가 {len(schema_files)}개다 — BC 당 정확히 하나다",
  4547	                where=bc.relative_to(root),
  4548	                msg=f"`bc_error_schema.py` 가 {len(schema_files)}개다 — BC 당 정확히 하나다",
  4549	            )
  4550	        for schema_file in schema_files:
  4551	            rel = schema_file.relative_to(root)
  4552	            try:
  4553	                mod = ast.parse(schema_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  4554	            except (SyntaxError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
  4555	                # "#N" 꼴 밖 표지 — findings.py 가 rule=null + sentinel 로 격리한다(parse-fail 류).
  4556	                findings.add(
  4557	                    rule="분석",
  4558	                    line=f"  [분석] {rel}: bc_error_schema.py 를 파싱하지 못했다",
  4559	                    where=rel,
  4560	                    msg="bc_error_schema.py 를 파싱하지 못했다",
  4561	                )
  4562	                continue
  4563	            classes = [n for n in mod.body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef)]
  4564	            schemas = [c for c in classes if c.name.endswith("ErrorSchema")]
  4565	            codes = [c for c in classes if c.name.endswith("ErrorCode")]
  4566	            strenum_names = {"StrEnum"}  # `from enum import StrEnum as X` 별칭도 StrEnum 이다
  4567	            for n in mod.body:
  4568	                if isinstance(n, ast.ImportFrom) and n.module == "enum":
  4569	                    strenum_names.update(a.asname or a.name for a in n.names if a.name == "StrEnum")
  4570	            if not schemas and not codes:
  4571	                continue  # 빈 파일 — HTTP 오류를 안 여는 BC(#114 문면)
  4572	            expected_schema = f"{expected_prefix}ErrorSchema"
  4573	            expected_code = f"{expected_prefix}ErrorCode"
  4574	            if not any(c.name == expected_schema for c in schemas):
  4575	                findings.add(
  4576	                    rule="#572",
  4577	                    line=f"  [#572] {rel}: 응답 본문 클래스 `{expected_schema}` 가 없다 — `<Bc>ErrorCode` 와 함께 온다(코드는 스키마 code 필드의 «타입»)",
  4578	                    where=rel,
  4579	                    msg=f"응답 본문 클래스 `{expected_schema}` 가 없다 — `<Bc>ErrorCode` 와 함께 온다(코드는 스키마 code 필드의 «타입»)",
  4580	                )
  4581	            if not any(c.name == expected_code for c in codes):
  4582	                findings.add(
  4583	                    rule="#572",
  4584	                    line=f"  [#572] {rel}: 오류 코드 `{expected_code}` 가 없다 — `<Bc>ErrorSchema` 와 함께 온다(떼면 둘이 따로 는다)",
  4585	                    where=rel,
  4586	                    msg=f"오류 코드 `{expected_code}` 가 없다 — `<Bc>ErrorSchema` 와 함께 온다(떼면 둘이 따로 는다)",
  4587	                )
  4588	            for code_cls in codes:
  4589	                base_names = {b.attr if isinstance(b, ast.Attribute) else getattr(b, "id", "") for b in code_cls.bases}
  4590	                if not (base_names & strenum_names):
  4591	                    findings.add(
  4592	                        rule="#636",
  4593	                        line=f"  [#636] {rel}: `{code_cls.name}` 는 `StrEnum` 이어야 한다 — Literal·맨 문자열 상수 모음으로 대신하지 않는다",
  4594	                        where=rel,
  4595	                        msg=f"`{code_cls.name}` 는 `StrEnum` 이어야 한다 — Literal·맨 문자열 상수 모음으로 대신하지 않는다",
  4596	                    )
  4597	    return findings

  4600	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
  4601	    try:
  4602	        config = _parse_config(argv[1:])
  4603	    except UsageError as exc:
  4604	        print(f"[check-error-centralization] 사용 오류: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
  4605	        return 1
  4606	    except SystemExit as exc:
  4607	        return int(exc.code)
  4608	
  4609	    if config.anchor is not None:
  4610	        # 재료 선검증 — 무발견 clean 이라도 resolve 불능 앵커·부재/형식 오류 빚 파일·
  4611	        # 공허 차분이 침묵 exit 0 되지 않게 parse 직후 막는다(fail-closed).
  4612	        try:
  4613	            anchor_diff.validate_materials(config.root, config.anchor, config.anchor_debt_file)
  4614	        except anchor_diff.AnchorDiffUsage as exc:
  4615	            print(f"[check-error-centralization] 사용 오류: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
  4616	            return 1
  4617	
  4618	    # 표준 트리 슬라이스 — 프로필과 무관하게 먼저 본다(옛 auto 무동작 = fail-open 차단).
  4619	    bcs = _tree_bcs(config.root)
  4620	    if not any(
  4621	        (bc / d).is_dir() for bc in bcs for d in _TREE_DRIVING
  4622	    ) and _tree_adopted(bcs):
  4623	        print("[check-error-centralization] blocker: 채택 신호는 있는데 driving 층이 0건이다 — 조용한 무동작을 금지한다(#74)")
  4624	        return 2
  4625	    # --anchor 미지정이면 현행 그대로 각 슬라이스에서 즉시 exit 2 — 지정 시에만
  4626	    # 슬라이스 진단을 모아 마지막에 판정 차분(anchor_diff)으로 exit 를 정한다.
  4627	    collected: list[str] = []
  4628	    pending_analysis: list[str] = []
  4629	    tree_findings = _tree_slice(config.root, bcs)
  4630	    if tree_findings:
  4631	        print("[check-error-centralization] BLOCKER — BC 오류 스키마 동거 규율 위반 (트리 10행):")
  4632	        for f in tree_findings:
  4633	            print(f)
  4634	        if config.anchor is None:
  4635	            return 2
  4636	        collected.extend(tree_findings)
  4637	
  4638	    if config.profile not in {None, "auto"}:
  4639	        try:
  4640	            inventory = _production_inventory(config.root)
  4641	            source_paths, code_paths, preserve_paths, plan_analysis, blockers = _source_plan(
  4642	                config, inventory
  4643	            )
  4644	            parsed, source_analysis = _load_sources(config.root, source_paths)
  4645	            analysis = [*plan_analysis, *source_analysis]
  4646	            findings: list[Finding] = []
  4647	            if config.profile == "dddjango-code-json":
  4648	                semantic_analysis, findings, blockers = _schema_findings(
  4649	                    config,
  4650	                    inventory,
  4651	                    parsed,
  4652	                    code_paths,
  4653	                    preserve_paths,
  4654	                    blockers,
  4655	                )
  4656	                analysis.extend(semantic_analysis)
  4657	            dynamic_proof_only = bool(analysis) and all(
  4658	                issue.startswith("DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED ")
  4659	                for issue in analysis
  4660	            )
  4661	            reported_findings = findings
  4662	            if dynamic_proof_only:
  4663	                # 정적 error shape 해석에 종속된 판정은 토큰-only 실행에서 억제하고
  4664	                # runtime proof 가 대신 검증한다(#15 requires_static_error_shape 동형).
  4665	                reported_findings = [
  4666	                    finding
  4667	                    for finding in findings
  4668	                    if not finding.requires_static_error_shape
  4669	                ]
  4670	            if (reported_findings or blockers) and (not analysis or dynamic_proof_only):
  4671	                print(
  4672	                    "[check-error-centralization] BLOCKER — code-profile FrameworkErrorSchema schema, "
  4673	                    "inventory, or raw code contract violation:"
  4674	                )
  4675	                # 레코드는 출력 대상과 1:1 거울 — 라인 문면은 blocker/render() 소유 그대로

  4677	                record_findings: ContractFindings = ContractFindings(CONTRACT_REF)
  4678	                for blocker in blockers:

  4681	                for finding in reported_findings:
  4682	                    print(finding.render())
  4683	                    record_findings.add(

  4685	                        where=f"{finding.relative_path}:{finding.lineno}",
  4686	                        msg=f"{finding.category}: {finding.shown}",
  4687	                        symbol=finding.category,
  4688	                    )
  4689	                if dynamic_proof_only:
  4690	                    # 토큰 진단을 삼키지 않는다(정보 손실 0) — exit 는 findings 가 정하되,
  4691	                    # 남은 정적 해석 불능이 «동적 error shape» 유형임을 함께 보고한다.
  4692	                    print(

  4694	                        "runtime proof 경로가 적용된다:)"
  4695	                    )
  4696	                    for issue in sorted(set(analysis)):
  4697	                        print(f"  - {issue}")
  4698	                print(
  4699	                    "  근거: common FrameworkErrorSchema는 프로젝트에서 승인한 exact wire shape의 단일 기반이고, 각 BC는 "
  4700	                    "canonical ErrorCode/FrameworkErrorSchema hierarchy와 project-wide unique wire code를 소유한다."
  4701	                )
  4702	                if config.anchor is None:
  4703	                    return 2
  4704	                collected.extend(f"  - {blocker}" for blocker in blockers)
  4705	                collected.extend(finding.render() for finding in reported_findings)
  4706	                if dynamic_proof_only:
  4707	                    # 토큰-only analysis 는 차분 강등으로 소거되지 않는다 — findings 가
  4708	                    # 전건 앵커 기존분이어도 proof 경로(exit 1)로 남긴다(fail-open 차단).
  4709	                    pending_analysis = sorted(set(analysis))
  4710	            elif analysis:

   692	def _expression_dotted_name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
   693	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
   694	        return node.id
   695	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
   696	        owner = _expression_dotted_name(node.value)
   697	        if owner is not None:
   698	            return f"{owner}.{node.attr}"
   699	    return None

  1348	def _registrar_spec(relative_path: Path) -> RegistrarSpec:
  1349	    parts = relative_path.parts
  1350	    if (
  1351	        len(parts) != 5
  1352	        or parts[0] != "application"
  1353	        or parts[2:] != ("driving_layer", "api", "api_router.py")
  1354	        or not parts[1].isidentifier()
  1355	    ):
  1356	        raise UsageError(
  1357	            "canonical registrar placement 분석 불능: "
  1358	            f"{relative_path} (application/<bc>/driving_layer/api/api_router.py 필요)"
  1359	        )

  1361	    function_name = f"register_{bc}_api"
  1362	    return RegistrarSpec(

  1367	def _analyze_registrar(
  1368	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  1369	    spec: RegistrarSpec,
  1370	    selected_api_module: str,
  1371	    analysis: list[str],
  1372	    findings: list[Finding],
  1373	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  1374	) -> None:
  1375	    facts = _all_import_facts(parsed)
  1376	    for fact in facts:
  1377	        if (
  1378	            fact.module == selected_api_module
  1379	            or fact.module.startswith(f"{selected_api_module}.")
  1380	            or fact.full_path == selected_api_module
  1381	            or fact.full_path.startswith(f"{selected_api_module}.")
  1382	        ):
  1383	            node = next(
  1384	                (item for item in ast.walk(parsed.tree) if getattr(item, "lineno", None) == fact.lineno),
  1385	                parsed.tree,
  1386	            )
  1387	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, "registrar imports selected project API")
  1388	
  1389	    public_functions = [
  1390	        node
  1391	        for node in parsed.tree.body
  1392	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
  1393	        and not node.name.startswith("_")
  1394	    ]
  1395	    canonical_nodes = [node for node in public_functions if node.name == spec.function_name]
  1396	    for node in public_functions:
  1397	        if node.name != spec.function_name:
  1398	            _append_finding(
  1399	                findings, seen, parsed, node, "additional public registrar function"
  1400	            )
  1401	    canonical: ast.FunctionDef | None = None
  1402	    if len(canonical_nodes) != 1 or not isinstance(canonical_nodes[0], ast.FunctionDef):
  1403	        anchor: ast.AST = canonical_nodes[0] if canonical_nodes else parsed.tree
  1404	        _append_finding(
  1405	            findings,
  1406	            seen,
  1407	            parsed,
  1408	            anchor,
  1409	            f"exactly one sync {spec.function_name} function required",
  1410	        )
  1411	    else:
  1412	        canonical = canonical_nodes[0]
  1413	
  1414	    parents = _parent_map(parsed.tree)
  1415	    allowed_calls: list[ast.Call] = []
  1416	    parameter_name: str | None = None
  1417	    if canonical is not None:
  1418	        arguments = canonical.args
  1419	        positional = [*arguments.posonlyargs, *arguments.args]
  1420	        valid_signature = (
  1421	            len(positional) == 1
  1422	            and not arguments.defaults
  1423	            and not arguments.kwonlyargs
  1424	            and arguments.vararg is None
  1425	            and arguments.kwarg is None
  1426	        )
  1427	        if valid_signature:
  1428	            parameter_name = positional[0].arg
  1429	        else:
  1430	            _append_finding(
  1431	                findings, seen, parsed, canonical, "registrar signature must be one required positional parameter"
  1432	            )
  1433	
  1434	    parameter_states = (
  1435	        _registrar_parameter_states(
  1436	            canonical,
  1437	            parameter_name,
  1438	            annotations_evaluated=_annotations_are_evaluated(parsed.tree),
  1439	        )
  1440	        if canonical is not None and parameter_name is not None
  1441	        else {}
  1442	    )
  1443	    for call in _direct_registration_calls(parsed):
  1444	        direct_owner = _expression_dotted_name(call.func.value)
  1445	        parameter_state = parameter_states.get(id(call))
  1446	        allowed = (
  1447	            canonical is not None
  1448	            and parameter_name is not None
  1449	            and direct_owner == parameter_name
  1450	            and _canonical_lexical_owner(call, canonical, parsed.tree, parents)
  1451	            and parameter_state == "incoming"
  1452	        )
  1453	        if allowed:
  1454	            allowed_calls.append(call)
  1455	        elif (
  1456	            canonical is not None
  1457	            and parameter_name is not None
  1458	            and direct_owner == parameter_name
  1459	            and _canonical_lexical_owner(call, canonical, parsed.tree, parents)
  1460	            and parameter_state == "ambiguous"
  1461	        ):
  1462	            analysis.append(
  1463	                "canonical registrar API parameter provenance 분석 불능: "
  1464	                f"{spec.function_name}:{call.lineno}"
  1465	            )
  1466	        else:
  1467	            _append_finding(
  1468	                findings, seen, parsed, call, "register_controllers outside canonical registrar owner"
  1469	            )
  1470	    for decorator in _bare_registration_decorators(parsed):
  1471	        _append_finding(
  1472	            findings, seen, parsed, decorator, "register_controllers decorator side effect"
  1473	        )
  1474	    if canonical is not None and parameter_name is not None and not allowed_calls:
  1475	        _append_finding(
  1476	            findings, seen, parsed, canonical, "canonical registrar has no direct register_controllers call"
  1477	        )
  1478	
  1479	
  1480	def _previous_import_resolves(
  1481	    node: ast.AST, facts: list[ImportFact], expected: str, before_line: int
  1482	) -> bool:
  1483	    dotted = _expression_dotted_name(node)
  1484	    if dotted is None:
  1485	        return False
  1486	    first, *rest = dotted.split(".")
  1487	    return any(
  1488	        fact.local_name == first
  1489	        and fact.lineno < before_line
  1490	        and ".".join((fact.binding, *rest)) == expected
  1491	        for fact in facts
  1492	    )
  1493	
  1494	
  1495	def _analyze_urlconf(
  1496	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  1497	    specs: list[RegistrarSpec],
  1498	    selected_api_object: str,
  1499	    analysis: list[str],
  1500	    findings: list[Finding],
  1501	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  1502	) -> None:
  1503	    before, invalidated_before, facts = _import_state(parsed)
  1504	    required = [selected_api_object, *(spec.full_path for spec in specs)]
  1505	    for target in required:
  1506	        if not any(_fact_covers(fact, target) for fact in facts):
  1507	            analysis.append(f"URLconf required import provenance 분석 불능: {target}")
  1508	
  1509	    parents = _parent_map(parsed.tree)
  1510	    events: dict[str, list[ast.Call]] = {spec.full_path: [] for spec in specs}
  1511	    expected_paths = set(events)
  1512	    for call in (node for node in ast.walk(parsed.tree) if isinstance(node, ast.Call)):
  1513	        top = _top_statement(call, parsed.tree, parents)
  1514	        bindings = before.get(id(top), {}) if top is not None else {}
  1515	        invalidated = (
  1516	            invalidated_before.get(id(top), frozenset()) if top is not None else frozenset()
  1517	        )
  1518	        resolved = _resolve_imported_expression(call.func, bindings, invalidated)
  1519	        if resolved not in expected_paths:
  1520	            for expected in expected_paths:
  1521	                if _previous_import_resolves(call.func, facts, expected, call.lineno):
  1522	                    analysis.append(
  1523	                        f"URLconf registrar provenance shadow/rebinding 분석 불능: {expected}:{call.lineno}"
  1524	                    )
  1525	            continue
  1526	        if not _is_module_direct_call(call, parsed.tree, parents):
  1527	            _append_finding(
  1528	                findings, seen, parsed, call, "registrar call must be a module-level direct event"
  1529	            )
  1530	            continue
  1531	        events[resolved].append(call)
  1532	        if len(call.args) != 1 or call.keywords or isinstance(call.args[0], ast.Starred):
  1533	            _append_finding(
  1534	                findings, seen, parsed, call, "registrar URLconf call has wrong arity"
  1535	            )
  1536	            continue
  1537	        resolved_argument = _resolve_imported_expression(call.args[0], bindings, invalidated)
  1538	        if resolved_argument != selected_api_object:
  1539	            analysis.append(
  1540	                "URLconf registrar-call API object identity 분석 불능: "
  1541	                f"{resolved}:{call.lineno}"
  1542	            )
  1543	
  1544	    for spec in specs:
  1545	        count = len(events[spec.full_path])
  1546	        if count != 1:
  1547	            anchor: ast.AST = events[spec.full_path][0] if events[spec.full_path] else parsed.tree
  1548	            _append_finding(
  1549	                findings,
  1550	                seen,
  1551	                parsed,
  1552	                anchor,
  1553	                f"{spec.function_name} must be called exactly once (actual {count})",
  1554	            )
  1555	
  1556	
  1557	def _composition_semantics(
  1558	    config: Config, parsed: dict[Path, ParsedSource]
  1559	) -> tuple[list[str], list[Finding]]:
  1560	    analysis: list[str] = []
  1561	    findings: list[Finding] = []
  1562	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]] = set()
  1563	    specs: list[RegistrarSpec] = []
  1564	    for raw in config.registrar_modules:
  1565	        try:
  1566	            specs.append(_registrar_spec(Path(raw)))
  1567	        except UsageError as exc:
  1568	            analysis.append(str(exc))
  1569	
  1570	    api_path = Path(config.api_module or "")
  1571	    api_source = parsed.get(api_path)
  1572	    selected_api_object: str | None = None
  1573	    if api_source is not None:
  1574	        try:
  1575	            selected_api_object = _required_root_api_object(api_source)
  1576	        except UsageError as exc:
  1577	            analysis.append(str(exc))
  1578	        for call in _direct_registration_calls(api_source):
  1579	            _append_finding(
  1580	                findings, seen, api_source, call, "register_controllers outside canonical registrar owner"
  1581	            )
  1582	        for decorator in _bare_registration_decorators(api_source):
  1583	            _append_finding(
  1584	                findings, seen, api_source, decorator, "register_controllers decorator side effect"
  1585	            )
  1586	
  1587	    selected_api_module = _module_name(api_path)
  1588	    for spec in specs:
  1589	        source = parsed.get(spec.relative_path)
  1590	        if source is not None:
  1591	            _analyze_registrar(
  1592	                source, spec, selected_api_module, analysis, findings, seen
  1593	            )
  1594	
  1595	    urlconf = parsed.get(Path(config.urlconf_module or ""))
  1596	    if urlconf is not None:
  1597	        for call in _direct_registration_calls(urlconf):
  1598	            _append_finding(
  1599	                findings, seen, urlconf, call, "register_controllers outside canonical registrar owner"
  1600	            )
  1601	        for decorator in _bare_registration_decorators(urlconf):
  1602	            _append_finding(
  1603	                findings, seen, urlconf, decorator, "register_controllers decorator side effect"
  1604	            )
  1605	        if selected_api_object is not None and len(specs) == len(config.registrar_modules):
  1606	            _analyze_urlconf(
  1607	                urlconf, specs, selected_api_object, analysis, findings, seen
  1608	            )
  1609	    return sorted(set(analysis)), sorted(
  1610	        findings, key=lambda item: (item.relative_path.as_posix(), item.lineno, item.category)
  1611	    )
  1612	
  1613	
  1614	# ── 표준 트리 슬라이스 — 트리 개정 명세 몫 18규칙 (트리 2~4·8·9·136·137행) ──
  1615	#
  1616	# 새 트리 모양(composition_root/ 폴더 · api/api_router.py · <project>/{api,urls}.py)에
  1617	# 대한 기계 규칙. 변종(단일 composition_root.py · off-tree composition/)은
  1618	# 위 V1~V3·registrar 검사가 담당한다.
  1619	#   #84/#497 composition_root/ 는 BC 루트의 «폴더»·결선 하나=파일 하나
  1620	#   #85/#86(ⓓ) dependency_wiring.py 는 build_* 팩토리만 · 조건/계산은 후보
  1621	#   #498/#500/#501 event_wiring.py 는 꽂기만(표 금지·이름 있는 최상단 함수만·DB 금지)
  1622	#   #101 BC 안쪽·composition_root 은 driving 층을 import 하지 않는다
  1623	#   #105/#112 api/ 직계 파일 둘뿐 · 등록 파일 이름은 api_router.py(접두 금지)
  1624	#   #107/#108/#109/#111 api_router.py 의 등록 함수 하나·전역 API import 금지·
  1625	#        top-level 부작용 등록 금지·등록 밖 일 금지
  1626	#   #437 <project>/api.py 닫힌 허용 목록 · #440/#441 <project>/urls.py 등록만
  1627	#   #511(ⓓ) api/ 2차 축 — 계약 소유(OAuth 콜백은 webhook/<provider>/) 후보
  1628	
  1629	_DRIVING_SEGMENTS = frozenset(
  1630	    {"driving_layer"}
  1680	        msg = "dependency_wiring.py 최상단에는 import 와 build_* 팩토리만 온다"
  1681	        findings.add("#85", line=f"  [#85] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1682	
  1683	
  1684	def _check_event_wiring(f: Path, rel: Path, findings: SliceFindings) -> None:
  1685	    mod = _slice_parse(f)
  1686	    if mod is None:
  1687	        return
  1688	    for node in ast.walk(mod):
  1689	        if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
  1690	            where: str = f"{rel}:{node.lineno}"
  1691	            msg: str = "event_wiring.py 에서 표(dict)를 만들었다 — 표는 event_subscription/event_router.py 소유, 여기는 브로커에 «꽂는» 것만 한다"
  1692	            findings.add("#498", line=f"  [#498] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1693	        elif isinstance(node, ast.Lambda):
  1694	            where = f"{rel}:{node.lineno}"
  1695	            msg = "구독으로 람다를 넘겼다 — 모듈 최상단 이름 있는 함수만(매번 다른 객체라 멱등이 깨진다)"
  1696	            findings.add("#500", line=f"  [#500] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1697	        elif isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  1698	            fn = node.func
  1699	            nm = fn.attr if isinstance(fn, ast.Attribute) else getattr(fn, "id", "")
  1700	            if nm == "partial":
  1701	                where = f"{rel}:{node.lineno}"
  1702	                msg = "`functools.partial` 을 구독으로 넘겼다 — 모듈 최상단 이름 있는 함수만"
  1703	                findings.add("#500", line=f"  [#500] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1704	        elif isinstance(node, ast.Attribute) and node.attr == "objects":
  1705	            where = f"{rel}:{node.lineno}"
  1706	            msg = "event_wiring.py 에서 DB 를 만졌다 — 모든 관리 명령에서 도는 자리다"
  1707	            findings.add("#501", line=f"  [#501] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1708	
  1709	
  1710	def _check_composition_dir(bc: Path, bc_rel: Path, findings: SliceFindings, candidates: SliceFindings) -> None:
  1711	    comp = bc / "composition_root"
  1712	    if not comp.is_dir():
  1713	        # 오배치(#84) — 층 폴더 안 composition_root/ 를 찾는다
  1714	        for layer in LAYER_DIRS:
  1715	            nested = bc / layer
  1716	            if not nested.is_dir():
  1717	                continue
  1718	            for p in nested.rglob("composition_root"):
  1719	                if p.is_dir():
  1720	                    nested_rel: Path = bc_rel / p.relative_to(bc)
  1721	                    nested_msg: str = "`composition_root/` 는 BC 루트에 둔다 — 네 층 폴더 어디에도 두지 않는다"
  1722	                    findings.add("#84", line=f"  [#84] {nested_rel}: {nested_msg}", where=nested_rel, msg=nested_msg)
  1723	        return
  1724	    for p in sorted(comp.iterdir()):
  1725	        if p.name.startswith(".") or p.name == "__pycache__":
  1726	            continue
  1727	        if p.is_dir():
  1728	            where: str = f"{bc_rel}/composition_root/{p.name}"
  1729	            msg: str = "폴더 금지 — «결선 하나 = 파일 하나»(지금은 dependency_wiring.py 와 event_wiring.py 둘)"
  1730	            findings.add("#497", line=f"  [#497] {where}: {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1731	        elif p.suffix == ".py" and p.name != "__init__.py" and p.name not in _WIRING_FILES:
  1732	            where = f"{bc_rel}/composition_root/{p.name}"
  1733	            msg = "결선 파일은 dependency_wiring.py·event_wiring.py 둘이다"
  1734	            findings.add("#497", line=f"  [#497] {where}: {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1735	    dep = comp / "dependency_wiring.py"
  1736	    if dep.is_file():
  1737	        _check_dependency_wiring(dep, bc_rel / "composition_root/dependency_wiring.py", findings, candidates)
  1738	    ev = comp / "event_wiring.py"
  1739	    if ev.is_file():
  1740	        _check_event_wiring(ev, bc_rel / "composition_root/event_wiring.py", findings)
  1741	
  1742	
  1743	def _check_inner_driving_imports(bc: Path, bc_rel: Path, findings: SliceFindings) -> None:
  1744	    for seg in _INNER_SEGMENTS:
  1745	        base = bc / seg
  1746	        files: list[Path] = []
  1747	        if base.is_dir():
  1748	            files = [p for p in base.rglob("*.py") if not (set(p.parts) & CODE_SKIP_DIRS) and "test" not in p.parts]
  1749	        elif seg == "composition_root" and (bc / "composition_root.py").is_file():
  1750	            files = [bc / "composition_root.py"]
  1751	        for f in files:
  1752	            mod = _slice_parse(f)
  1753	            if mod is None:
  1754	                continue
  1755	            for lineno, path_str in _slice_imports(mod):
  1756	                if set(path_str.split(".")) & _DRIVING_SEGMENTS:
  1757	                    where: str = f"{bc_rel / f.relative_to(bc)}:{lineno}"
  1758	                    msg: str = f"`{path_str}` — BC 안쪽과 composition_root 은 driving 층을 import 하지 않는다(예외 없음 · rd-2)"
  1759	                    findings.add("#101", line=f"  [#101] {where} {msg}", where=where, msg=msg)
  1760
